    """PostgreSQL schema and transactional ingestion helpers."""
    
    import hashlib
    import json
    import os
    import uuid
    from contextlib import contextmanager
    from urllib.parse import urlparse
    
    import psycopg2
    from psycopg2.extras import execute_values
    
    
    def _db_config():
        """Use libpq-compatible environment settings, with legacy defaults."""
        return {
            "dbname": os.environ.get("PGDATABASE", "warcdb"),
            "user": os.environ.get("PGUSER", "warc_user"),
            "password": os.environ.get("PGPASSWORD", "password"),
            "host": os.environ.get("PGHOST", "localhost"),
            "port": os.environ.get("PGPORT", "5432"),
            "connect_timeout": int(os.environ.get("PGCONNECT_TIMEOUT", "10")),
        }
    
    
    DB_CONFIG = _db_config()
    
    
    def get_connection():
        return psycopg2.connect(**_db_config())
    
    
    @contextmanager
    def _cursor(conn):
        cur = conn.cursor()
        try:
            yield cur
        finally:
            cur.close()
    
    
    def init_db():
        """Apply additive, backwards-compatible schema migrations."""
        ddl = """
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS websites (
            id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, domain TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS authors (
            id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pages (
            id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
            website_id INT REFERENCES websites(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            author_id INT REFERENCES authors(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
            warc_record_id TEXT UNIQUE, url TEXT NOT NULL,
            source_file_url TEXT, warc_date TEXT,
            created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata (
            page_id INT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES pages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            title TEXT, published_date TEXT, language TEXT, arabic_dialect TEXT,
            word_count INT, char_count INT, links_count INT, headings JSONB,
            extraction_method TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS content (
            page_id INT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES pages(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            cleaned_text TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ingest_files (
            file_url TEXT PRIMARY KEY, manifest_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            bytes_done BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            articles_done INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            complete BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ingest_runs (
            run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY, manifest_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            target_articles BIGINT NOT NULL, per_file_target BIGINT NOT NULL,
            inserted_count BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running',
            config JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
            created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            CHECK (target_articles >= 0), CHECK (per_file_target >= 0));
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ingest_run_files (
            run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ingest_runs(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            ordinal INT NOT NULL, file_url TEXT NOT NULL,
            next_offset BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            inserted_count BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            eof BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
            etag TEXT, last_modified TEXT, last_error TEXT,
            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            PRIMARY KEY (run_id, file_url), UNIQUE (run_id, ordinal));
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ingest_chunks (
            chunk_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES ingest_runs(run_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
            file_url TEXT NOT NULL, start_offset BIGINT NOT NULL,
            next_offset BIGINT NOT NULL, candidate_count INT NOT NULL,
            inserted_count INT NOT NULL, eof BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
            stats JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
            committed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ingest_files_manifest ON ingest_files (manifest_id);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ingest_run_files_sched
            ON ingest_run_files (run_id, status, ordinal);
        ALTER TABLE pages ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS author_id INT REFERENCES authors(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
        ALTER TABLE pages ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS source_file_url TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE pages ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS warc_date TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE metadata ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS arabic_dialect TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE metadata ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS extraction_method TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE websites ALTER COLUMN domain TYPE TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE authors ALTER COLUMN name TYPE TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE pages ALTER COLUMN warc_record_id TYPE TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE metadata ALTER COLUMN published_date TYPE TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE metadata ALTER COLUMN language TYPE TEXT;
        ALTER TABLE metadata ALTER COLUMN arabic_dialect TYPE TEXT;
        """
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute(ddl)
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def extract_domain(url):
        try:
            host = (urlparse(url).hostname or "").lower().rstrip(".")
            if host.startswith("www."):
                host = host[4:]
            return host or "unknown_domain"
        except Exception:
            return "unknown_domain"
    
    
    def acquire_coordinator_lock(manifest_id):
        """Return a live connection holding a manifest-scoped advisory lock."""
        conn = get_connection()
        key = int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(manifest_id.encode()).digest()[:8], "big", signed=True)
        with _cursor(conn) as cur:
            cur.execute("SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(%s);", (key,))
            locked = cur.fetchone()[0]
        if not locked:
            conn.close()
            raise RuntimeError(f"another ingestion coordinator is active for {manifest_id}")
        return conn
    
    
    def create_run(manifest_id, urls, target_articles, per_file_target, config):
        run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO ingest_runs (run_id,manifest_id,target_articles,per_file_target,config) "
                    "VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s::jsonb)",
                    (run_id, manifest_id, target_articles, per_file_target, json.dumps(config)),
                )
                cur.execute("SELECT file_url FROM ingest_files WHERE manifest_id=%s AND complete", (manifest_id,))
                complete = {row[0] for row in cur.fetchall()}
                rows = [(run_id, i, url, url in complete, "eof" if url in complete else "pending")
                        for i, url in enumerate(urls)]
                if rows:
                    execute_values(cur, "INSERT INTO ingest_run_files (run_id,ordinal,file_url,eof,status) VALUES %s", rows)
            conn.commit()
            return run_id
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def load_run(run_id):
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT run_id::text,manifest_id,target_articles,per_file_target,inserted_count,status,config "
                    "FROM ingest_runs WHERE run_id=%s", (run_id,))
                row = cur.fetchone()
                if row is None:
                    raise ValueError(f"unknown RESUME_RUN_ID: {run_id}")
                keys = ("run_id", "manifest_id", "target_articles", "per_file_target", "inserted_count", "status", "config")
                return dict(zip(keys, row))
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def load_run_files(run_id):
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute(
                    "SELECT file_url,ordinal,next_offset,inserted_count,eof,status,etag,last_modified,last_error "
                    "FROM ingest_run_files WHERE run_id=%s ORDER BY ordinal", (run_id,))
                keys = ("file_url", "ordinal", "next_offset", "inserted_count", "eof", "status", "etag", "last_modified", "last_error")
                return [dict(zip(keys, row)) for row in cur.fetchall()]
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def _insert_new_records(cur, records):
        if not records:
            return []
        domains = sorted({extract_domain(r["url"]) for r in records})
        execute_values(cur, "INSERT INTO websites (domain) VALUES %s ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [(x,) for x in domains])
        cur.execute("SELECT domain,id FROM websites WHERE domain=ANY(%s)", (domains,))
        domain_ids = dict(cur.fetchall())
        authors = sorted({r.get("author", "").strip() for r in records
                          if r.get("author", "").strip() not in ("", "N/A")})
        author_ids = {}
        if authors:
            execute_values(cur, "INSERT INTO authors (name) VALUES %s ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [(x,) for x in authors])
            cur.execute("SELECT name,id FROM authors WHERE name=ANY(%s)", (authors,))
            author_ids = dict(cur.fetchall())
        rows = [(domain_ids[extract_domain(r["url"])], author_ids.get(r.get("author", "").strip()),
                 r["record_id"], r["url"], r["file_url"], r.get("warc_date")) for r in records]
        inserted = execute_values(
            cur,
            """INSERT INTO pages (website_id,author_id,warc_record_id,url,source_file_url,warc_date)
               VALUES %s ON CONFLICT (warc_record_id) DO NOTHING RETURNING warc_record_id,id""",
            rows, fetch=True)
        page_ids = {record_id: page_id for record_id, page_id in inserted}
        new_records = [r for r in records if r["record_id"] in page_ids]
        if new_records:
            execute_values(cur, """INSERT INTO metadata
                (page_id,title,published_date,language,arabic_dialect,word_count,char_count,links_count,headings,extraction_method)
                VALUES %s""", [(
                    page_ids[r["record_id"]], r.get("title"), r.get("published_date"), r.get("language"),
                    r.get("arabic_dialect"), r.get("word_count", 0), r.get("char_count", 0),
                    r.get("links_count", 0), json.dumps(r.get("headings", [])), r.get("extraction"),
                ) for r in new_records])
            execute_values(cur, "INSERT INTO content (page_id,cleaned_text) VALUES %s",
                           [(page_ids[r["record_id"]], r["cleaned_text"]) for r in new_records])
        return new_records
    
    
    def commit_chunk(run_id, result):
        """Atomically insert new articles and advance this run's file cursor."""
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT inserted_count FROM ingest_chunks WHERE chunk_id=%s", (result["chunk_id"],))
                prior = cur.fetchone()
                if prior is not None:
                    conn.rollback()
                    return prior[0]
                cur.execute("SELECT target_articles,per_file_target,inserted_count FROM ingest_runs WHERE run_id=%s FOR UPDATE", (run_id,))
                target, per_file, total_done = cur.fetchone()
                cur.execute("SELECT inserted_count FROM ingest_run_files WHERE run_id=%s AND file_url=%s FOR UPDATE",
                            (run_id, result["file_url"]))
                file_done = cur.fetchone()[0]
                total_left = len(result["candidates"]) if target == 0 else max(0, target - total_done)
                file_left = len(result["candidates"]) if per_file == 0 else max(0, per_file - file_done)
                candidates = result["candidates"][:min(len(result["candidates"]), total_left, file_left)]
                inserted_count = len(_insert_new_records(cur, candidates))
                failure, eof = result.get("error"), bool(result.get("eof"))
                status = "failed" if failure else ("eof" if eof else "pending")
                if per_file and file_done + inserted_count >= per_file:
                    status = "quota_reached"
                cur.execute("""UPDATE ingest_run_files SET next_offset=%s,inserted_count=inserted_count+%s,
                    eof=%s,status=%s,etag=COALESCE(%s,etag),last_modified=COALESCE(%s,last_modified),
                    last_error=%s,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE run_id=%s AND file_url=%s""",
                    (result["next_offset"], inserted_count, eof, status, result.get("etag"),
                     result.get("last_modified"), failure, run_id, result["file_url"]))
                cur.execute("UPDATE ingest_runs SET inserted_count=inserted_count+%s,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE run_id=%s",
                            (inserted_count, run_id))
                cur.execute("""INSERT INTO ingest_chunks
                    (chunk_id,run_id,file_url,start_offset,next_offset,candidate_count,inserted_count,eof,stats)
                    VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s::jsonb)""",
                    (result["chunk_id"], run_id, result["file_url"], result["start_offset"], result["next_offset"],
                     len(result["candidates"]), inserted_count, eof, json.dumps(result.get("stats", {}))))
                cur.execute("""INSERT INTO ingest_files (file_url,manifest_id,bytes_done,articles_done,complete)
                    SELECT %s,manifest_id,%s,%s,%s FROM ingest_runs WHERE run_id=%s
                    ON CONFLICT (file_url) DO UPDATE SET
                    bytes_done=GREATEST(ingest_files.bytes_done,EXCLUDED.bytes_done),
                    articles_done=ingest_files.articles_done+EXCLUDED.articles_done,
                    complete=ingest_files.complete OR EXCLUDED.complete,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP""",
                    (result["file_url"], result["next_offset"], inserted_count, eof, run_id))
            conn.commit()
            return inserted_count
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def finish_run(run_id, status):
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute("UPDATE ingest_runs SET status=%s,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE run_id=%s", (status, run_id))
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def save_batch_records(records_list, conn=None):
        """Compatibility helper; returns the number of newly inserted records."""
        own = conn is None
        conn = conn or get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                count = len(_insert_new_records(cur, records_list))
            conn.commit()
            return count
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
        finally:
            if own:
                conn.close()
    
    
    def load_ingest_progress(manifest_id):
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute("SELECT file_url,bytes_done,articles_done,complete FROM ingest_files WHERE manifest_id=%s", (manifest_id,))
                return {row[0]: row[1:] for row in cur.fetchall()}
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    def upsert_ingest_progress(manifest_id, file_url, bytes_done, articles_done, complete):
        conn = get_connection()
        try:
            with _cursor(conn) as cur:
                cur.execute("""INSERT INTO ingest_files (file_url,manifest_id,bytes_done,articles_done,complete)
                    VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s) ON CONFLICT (file_url) DO UPDATE SET
                    bytes_done=GREATEST(ingest_files.bytes_done,EXCLUDED.bytes_done),
                    articles_done=GREATEST(ingest_files.articles_done,EXCLUDED.articles_done),
                    complete=ingest_files.complete OR EXCLUDED.complete,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP""",
                    (file_url, manifest_id, bytes_done, articles_done, complete))
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            conn.close()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        init_db()

    import io
    from typing import Generator, Dict, Any, Optional
    
    
    def _readable_stream(value):
        if isinstance(value, (bytes, bytearray)):
            return io.BytesIO(value)
        if hasattr(value, "read"):
            return value
        raise TypeError(f"Unsupported WARC input type: {type(value)!r}")
    
    
    def parse_warc_records_streaming(stream) -> Generator[Dict[str, Any], None, None]:
        """Record-at-a-time streaming WARC parser.
    
        Parses each response record as its bytes arrive over a non-seekable
        stream (e.g. an open HTTP response). Holds O(one article) in memory and
        O(1) total — the file is never staged to disk or RAM. A truncated tail
        (connection close, early abort) ends iteration silently; complete records
        yielded so far are unaffected.
        """
        from warcio.archiveiterator import ArchiveIterator
    
        stream = _readable_stream(stream)
        try:
            iterator = ArchiveIterator(stream, arc2warc=True)
            for record in iterator:
                try:
                    if record.rec_type != "response":
                        continue
                    content_type = ""
                    if record.http_headers:
                        try:
                            content_type = record.http_headers.get_header("Content-Type") or ""
                        except Exception:
                            content_type = ""
                    if "text/html" not in content_type.lower():
                        continue
                    try:
                        raw_bytes = record.content_stream().read()
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    if not raw_bytes:
                        continue
                    headers = record.rec_headers
                    yield {
                        "url": headers.get_header("WARC-Target-URI"),
                        "warc_date": headers.get_header("WARC-Date"),
                        "content_type": content_type,
                        "record_id": headers.get_header("WARC-Record-ID"),
                        "raw_bytes": raw_bytes,
                    }
                except Exception:
                    continue
        except Exception:
            # Truncated stream tail (early close, cut connection): stop iteration,
            # keep the complete records already yielded.
            return
    
    
    def parse_warc_stream_fastwarc(stream, max_warc_bytes: Optional[int] = None, **kwargs) -> Generator[Dict[str, Any], None, None]:
        """Streaming entry point (name kept for backwards compatibility).
    
        Uses the warcio record-at-a-time path: no byte cap, no temp file.
        Extra args (e.g. legacy max_warc_bytes) are accepted and ignored.
        """
        _ = max_warc_bytes
        _ = kwargs
        yield from parse_warc_records_streaming(stream)

    """Bounded streaming WARC parsing backed by FastWARC."""
    
    from fastwarc.warc import ArchiveIterator, WarcRecordType
    
    
    class OversizedPayload(Exception):
        pass
    
    
    def parse_warc_records_streaming(stream, max_html_bytes=2 * 1024 * 1024, base_offset=0):
        """Yield HTML response records with exact compressed source offsets.
    
        Parser/transport failures deliberately propagate so callers can retry; they
        must never be confused with a clean EOF.
        """
        iterator = ArchiveIterator(
            stream,
            record_types=WarcRecordType.response,
            parse_http=True,
            auto_decode="all",
            max_header_len=64 * 1024,
            stream_detect=True,
            buffer_size=64 * 1024,
            fsspec_args=False,
        )
        for record in iterator:
            start_offset = base_offset + record.stream_pos
            content_type = (record.http_content_type or "").lower()
            if "text/html" not in content_type and "application/xhtml" not in content_type:
                yield {"skip": "non_html", "start_offset": start_offset}
                continue
            payload = record.reader.read(max_html_bytes + 1)
            if len(payload) > max_html_bytes:
                yield {"skip": "oversized_html", "start_offset": start_offset}
                continue
            yield {
                "url": record.headers.get("WARC-Target-URI") or "",
                "warc_date": record.headers.get("WARC-Date"),
                "content_type": content_type,
                "record_id": record.record_id or record.headers.get("WARC-Record-ID") or "",
                "raw_bytes": payload,
                "start_offset": start_offset,
            }
    
    
    def parse_warc_stream_fastwarc(stream, **kwargs):
        """Compatibility name for older callers; now genuinely uses FastWARC."""
        yield from parse_warc_records_streaming(stream, **kwargs)

## Assistant

The first implementation pass has landed for the database and extraction layers: run/chunk state is transactional, new-row counts come from `RETURNING`, French accents are no longer treated as corruption, JSON-LD is read before scripts are removed, and the parser now uses FastWARC with bounded payloads and real record offsets. I’m replacing the driver loop now so those pieces work together.

    import sys
    import os
    import time
    import json
    import hashlib
    import gzip
    import socket
    import urllib.request
    import urllib.error
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
    
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    if BASE_DIR not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, BASE_DIR)
    
    from extractors.extractors import extract_html_fields, detect_languages_batch
    from db.db_handler import (
        init_db, save_batch_records, get_connection,
        load_ingest_progress, upsert_ingest_progress,
    )
    from parsers.parsers import parse_warc_stream_fastwarc
    from utils import is_target_language
    
    YEAR = os.environ.get("YEAR", "2026")
    MONTH = os.environ.get("MONTH", "05")
    MANIFEST_ID = f"{YEAR}/{MONTH}"
    # Resume overlap: rewind this far before the stored byte offset so the
    # resync scan lands on a valid gzip member. Overlap duplicates collapse
    # driver-side via record_id (upsert-safe); the straddled boundary article
    # is the accepted loss. 0 disables byte resume (full GET every file).
    RESUME_OVERLAP_BYTES = int(os.environ.get("RESUME_OVERLAP_BYTES", str(1024 * 1024)))
    GZIP_MAGIC = b"\x1f\x8b"
    
    
    class _CountingReader:
        """File-like byte counter for worker-side resume offsets.
    
        Wraps the HTTP response; warcio reads through it while every byte is
        counted. A pushback buffer lets the gzip resync scan return the member
        header it stopped at.
        """
    
        def __init__(self, raw):
            self._raw = raw
            self._buf = b""
            self.bytes_read = 0
    
        def read(self, size=-1):
            if size is None or size < 0:
                chunks = [self._buf]
                self._buf = b""
                while True:
                    data = self._raw.read(65536)
                    if not data:
                        break
                    chunks.append(data)
                out = b"".join(chunks)
            else:
                while len(self._buf) < size:
                    data = self._raw.read(max(65536, size - len(self._buf)))
                    if not data:
                        break
                    self._buf += data
                out, self._buf = self._buf[:size], self._buf[size:]
            self.bytes_read += len(out)
            return out
    
        def pushback(self, data: bytes) -> None:
            self._buf = data + self._buf
            self.bytes_read -= len(data)
    
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            # Delegate anything else (close, seekable, ...) to the raw stream
            # so warcio sees the file-like it expects.
            return getattr(self.__dict__["_raw"], name)
    
    
    def _resync_gzip_member(reader: _CountingReader, max_scan: int = 8 * 1024 * 1024) -> bool:
        """Advance a Range-resumed stream to the next gzip member boundary.
    
        Returns True once the member header is pushed back for the parser.
        False when no valid boundary appears within max_scan (caller falls
        back to a full GET). Header check is magic + CM==deflate + reserved
        FLG bits clear; false positives are ~1e-8 per position.
        """
        window = b""
        scanned = 0
        while scanned < max_scan:
            chunk = reader.read(65536)
            if not chunk:
                return False
            window += chunk
            scanned += len(chunk)
            pos = 0
            while True:
                idx = window.find(GZIP_MAGIC, pos)
                if idx == -1:
                    window = window[-1:]  # keep tail split-magic bytes
                    break
                if idx + 4 > len(window):
                    window = window[idx:]  # need more bytes to validate
                    pos = 0
                    break
                cm, flg = window[idx + 2], window[idx + 3]
                if cm == 8 and (flg & 0xE0) == 0:
                    reader.pushback(window[idx:])
                    return True
                pos = idx + 1
        return False
    
    
    PATHS_URL = f"https://data.commoncrawl.org/crawl-data/CC-NEWS/{YEAR}/{MONTH}/warc.paths.gz"
    HEADERS = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"}
    DB_BATCH_SIZE = int(os.environ.get("DB_BATCH_SIZE", "25"))
    
    MAX_RETRIES = int(os.environ.get("MAX_RETRIES", "3"))
    DEAD_LETTER_DIR = os.environ.get("DEAD_LETTER_DIR", os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "dead_letter"))
    
    # Article caps. MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND <= 0 means unlimited transfer per
    # round (worker-visible extracted count, the per-round transfer chunk).
    # MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES <= 0 means unlimited accepted articles for the run.
    # NUM_FILES None means to the end of the manifest ("end of the paths file").
    # START_FILE_OFFSET skips the first N manifest entries (file-range targeting).
    MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND = int(os.environ.get("MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND", "2000"))
    # Per-file accepted cap (target-language + fresh, counted driver-side).
    # The worker can't know acceptance (HF runs on the driver), so each file is
    # processed in rounds: every round resumes from the stored byte checkpoint
    # and the driver stops re-submitting the file once its accepted count hits
    # this cap (0 = unlimited, file runs to EOF).
    MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE = int(os.environ.get("MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE", "0"))
    # Files per round: bounds in-flight partitions/task-results no matter how big
    # the manifest is (0 = all unfinished files every round). Default matches the
    # executor count so each executor typically hosts one file-task.
    MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND = int(os.environ.get("MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND", "6"))
    # Article = MIN_WORD_COUNT+ words after extraction (both the trafilatura
    # acceptance and the yield gate; passed to workers via task closure).
    MIN_WORD_COUNT = int(os.environ.get("MIN_WORD_COUNT", "80"))
    MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES = int(os.environ.get("MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES", "0"))
    # Driver progress bar (stderr, silent when piped). NO_PROGRESS=1 disables it.
    USE_PROGRESS_BAR = os.environ.get("NO_PROGRESS", "0") != "1"
    
    
    def _parse_num_files(raw: str | None) -> int | None:
        if raw is None:
            return 15
        text = raw.strip().lower()
        if text in ("", "all", "none"):
            return None
        try:
            value = int(text)
        except ValueError:
            return 15
        return None if value <= 0 else value
    
    
    NUM_FILES = _parse_num_files(os.environ.get("NUM_FILES"))
    START_FILE_OFFSET = max(0, int(os.environ.get("START_FILE_OFFSET", "0") or 0))
    
    
    def get_spark_session() -> SparkSession:
        try:
            existing_session = SparkSession.getActiveSession()
            if existing_session:
                return existing_session
        except Exception:
            pass
    
        builder = (
            SparkSession.builder
            .appName("CC-NEWS-Stream-To-DB-Distributed-Cluster")
            # Inherit resource limits from /opt/spark/conf/spark-defaults.conf
            # or spark-submit; this cluster has three workers with 2 GB RAM each.
            .config("spark.sql.execution.arrow.pyspark.enabled", "true")
            # Task results under this size ride back on the existing
            # executor->driver channel (direct). Larger ones are stored in the
            # executor BlockManager and fetched over a NEW driver->executor
            # connection, which this cluster refuses: keep results direct.
            .config("spark.task.maxDirectResultSize", "32m")
        )
        # Only force a master when explicitly requested. Otherwise inherit it
        # from spark-submit (--master yarn) so the job actually uses workers.
        # Direct Python invocation also loads this installation's Spark defaults.
        if "SPARK_MASTER_URL" in os.environ:
            builder = builder.master(os.environ["SPARK_MASTER_URL"])
        return builder.getOrCreate()
    
    
    def make_record_id(record, url, text):
        rid = record.get("record_id", "")
        if rid:
            return rid
        basis = (url or "") + (text[:200] if text else "")
        return "gen-" + hashlib.md5(basis.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()
    
    
    def _is_transient_download_error(error: Exception) -> bool:
        if isinstance(error, (socket.timeout, TimeoutError, ConnectionError)):
            return True
        if isinstance(error, urllib.error.HTTPError):
            return error.code == 429 or 500 <= error.code < 600
        if isinstance(error, urllib.error.URLError):
            return True
        return False
    
    
    def _sleep_backoff(attempt: int) -> None:
        time.sleep(2 ** (attempt + 1))
    
    
    def _write_dead_letter(batch: list, error: Exception) -> str:
        os.makedirs(DEAD_LETTER_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
        path = os.path.join(DEAD_LETTER_DIR, f"failed-batch-{stamp}-{os.getpid()}.jsonl")
        payload = {"error": f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}", "records": batch}
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(payload, fh, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
            fh.write("\n")
        return path
    
    
    def _filter_already_saved(conn, record_ids: list) -> set:
        """Return the subset of record_ids already present in pages."""
        if not record_ids:
            return set()
        cur = conn.cursor()
        try:
            cur.execute(
                "SELECT warc_record_id FROM pages WHERE warc_record_id = ANY(%s);",
                (record_ids,),
            )
            return {row[0] for row in cur.fetchall()}
        finally:
            cur.close()
    
    
    def _save_batch_with_retry(batch: list) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
        """Save one batch with reconnect + backoff. Returns (saved_count, dead_letter_path)."""
        last_error: Exception | None = None
        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
            conn = None
            try:
                conn = get_connection()
                saved_before = len(batch)
                save_batch_records(batch, conn=conn)
                return saved_before, None
            except Exception as error:
                last_error = error
                try:
                    if conn is not None:
                        conn.rollback()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                print(f"DB batch write failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {error}")
                if attempt < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
                    _sleep_backoff(attempt)
            finally:
                try:
                    if conn is not None:
                        conn.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass
        assert last_error is not None
        path = _write_dead_letter(batch, last_error)
        print(f"DB batch written to dead-letter file, continuing: {path}")
        return 0, path
    
    
    def _flush_batch(db_batch: list, seen_ids: set, stats: dict, file_accepted: dict) -> list:
        """Language-filter + resume-filter + save one accumulated batch. Returns []."""
        if not db_batch:
            return []
        conn = None
        try:
            conn = get_connection()
            already = _filter_already_saved(conn, [item["record_id"] for item in db_batch])
        except Exception as error:
            print(f"Resume pre-check failed, proceeding to upsert anyway: {error}")
            already = set()
        finally:
            try:
                if conn is not None:
                    conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
    
        fresh = []
        for item in db_batch:
            if item["record_id"] in already or item["record_id"] in seen_ids:
                stats["skipped_saved"] += 1
                continue
            fresh.append(item)
        if not fresh:
            return []
    
        texts = [item["cleaned_text"] for item in fresh]
        languages = detect_languages_batch(texts)
    
        valid_batch = []
        for item, lang in zip(fresh, languages):
            item["language"] = lang
            if is_target_language(lang):
                valid_batch.append(item)
                stats["accepted"] += 1
                furl = item.get("file_url", "")
                if furl:
                    file_accepted[furl] = file_accepted.get(furl, 0) + 1
            else:
                stats["rejected"] += 1
    
        if valid_batch:
            saved, dead_letter = _save_batch_with_retry(valid_batch)
            stats["stored"] += saved
            if dead_letter:
                stats["dead_letter"] += 1
            else:
                for item in valid_batch:
                    seen_ids.add(item["record_id"])
        else:
            for item in fresh:
                seen_ids.add(item["record_id"])
        return []
    
    
    def run_streaming_pipeline():
        spark = get_spark_session()
        sc = spark.sparkContext
        sc.setLogLevel("WARN")  # quiet Spark INFO flood; the progress bar owns the console
    
        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
            try:
                init_db()
                break
            except Exception as error:
                print(f"init_db failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {error}")
                if attempt == MAX_RETRIES - 1:
                    raise
                _sleep_backoff(attempt)
    
        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
            try:
                req = urllib.request.Request(PATHS_URL, headers=HEADERS)
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as response:
                    paths_list = gzip.decompress(response.read()).decode("utf-8").splitlines()
                break
            except Exception as error:
                print(f"Manifest fetch failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {error}")
                if attempt == MAX_RETRIES - 1:
                    raise
                _sleep_backoff(attempt)
    
        window = paths_list[START_FILE_OFFSET:]
        selected = window if NUM_FILES is None else window[:NUM_FILES]
        warc_urls = [f"https://data.commoncrawl.org/{p}" for p in selected]
        if not warc_urls:
            print("No WARC URLs to process.")
            spark.stop()
            return
    
        # --- Byte-range resume checkpoints (automatic, ingest_files table) ---
        # Completed files are skipped entirely; partial files resume from the
        # stored wire offset minus RESUME_OVERLAP_BYTES via HTTP Range. Overlap
        # duplicates collapse in _flush_batch via record_id.
        try:
            stored_progress = load_ingest_progress(MANIFEST_ID)
        except Exception as error:
            print(f"Checkpoint load failed, starting fresh: {error}")
            stored_progress = {}
        skipped_complete = 0
        resume_map = {}
        live_urls = []
        overlap = max(0, RESUME_OVERLAP_BYTES)
        for url in warc_urls:
            entry = stored_progress.get(url)
            if entry is not None and entry[2]:
                skipped_complete += 1
                continue
            live_urls.append(url)
            if entry is not None and entry[0] > 0:
                resume_map[url] = (
                    max(0, entry[0] - overlap),
                    entry[1],
                )
        warc_urls = live_urls
        if skipped_complete:
            print(f"Checkpoint: skipping {skipped_complete} completed files.")
        if resume_map:
            print(f"Checkpoint: resuming {len(resume_map)} partial files from stored offsets.")
        if not warc_urls:
            print("All selected files already complete, nothing to do.")
            spark.stop()
            return
    
        pending_new = {}    # file_url -> new articles since last commit
        pending_bytes = {}  # file_url -> max wire bytes since last commit
        touched = set()
    
        def _commit_progress(completed: dict | None = None) -> None:
            """Upsert pending per-file checkpoints (flush-then-commit order)."""
            if completed is None:
                completed = {}
            urls = set(touched) | set(completed)
            if not urls:
                return
            for url in urls:
                prev = stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))
                new_bytes = max(prev[0], pending_bytes.get(url, 0))
                new_articles = prev[1] + pending_new.get(url, 0)
                is_done = bool(prev[2] or completed.get(url, False))
                try:
                    upsert_ingest_progress(MANIFEST_ID, url, new_bytes, new_articles, is_done)
                except Exception as error:
                    print(f"Checkpoint write failed for {url}: {error}")
                    continue
                stored_progress[url] = (new_bytes, new_articles, is_done)
            for url in urls:
                pending_new.pop(url, None)
                pending_bytes.pop(url, None)
            touched.clear()
    
        chunk_cap = MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND if MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND > 0 else None
        # Per-round resume map. The driver rewrites round_state["resume"] before
        # submitting each round's job; tasks serialize its current contents.
        # chunk_cap bounds transfer per round (worker-visible extracted count).
        round_state = {"resume": {}}
    
        def _total_size(response, ranged: bool):
            """Best-effort compressed file size for EOF detection (None if unknown)."""
            try:
                if ranged:
                    content_range = response.headers.get("Content-Range", "")
                    # e.g. "bytes 1048576-5242879/5242880"
                    total = content_range.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
                    return int(total) if total.isdigit() else None
                length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
                return int(length) if length and length.isdigit() else None
            except Exception:
                return None
    
        def process_partition(iterator):
            for file_url in iterator:
                stored_bytes, stored_articles = round_state["resume"].get(file_url, (0, 0))
                file_count = 0
                range_failed = False  # one fallback to full GET per file max
                for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
                    response = None
                    try:
                        ranged = stored_bytes > 0 and not range_failed
                        headers = dict(HEADERS)
                        if ranged:
                            headers["Range"] = f"bytes={stored_bytes}-"
                        stream_req = urllib.request.Request(file_url, headers=headers)
                        response = urllib.request.urlopen(stream_req, timeout=120)
                        status = getattr(response, "status", 200)
                        counter = _CountingReader(response)
                        wire_base = stored_bytes if ranged and status != 200 else 0
                        fallback_skip = 0
                        if ranged and status == 200:
                            # Server ignored Range: parse all, skip extraction
                            # for the previously yielded prefix by count.
                            print(f"Range ignored for {file_url}, falling back to count-skip.")
                            fallback_skip = stored_articles
                        elif ranged:
                            if not _resync_gzip_member(counter):
                                print(f"Resync failed for {file_url}, falling back to full GET.")
                                range_failed = True
                                try:
                                    response.close()
                                except Exception:
                                    pass
                                continue
                            wire_base = stored_bytes
                        total_size = _total_size(response, ranged and status != 200)
                        parsed_seen = 0
                        capped = False
                        for raw_rec in parse_warc_stream_fastwarc(counter):
                            parsed_seen += 1
                            if fallback_skip and parsed_seen <= fallback_skip:
                                continue
                            try:
                                fields = extract_html_fields(raw_rec, MIN_WORD_COUNT)
                            except Exception as error:
                                print(f"Skipping bad record in {file_url}: {error}")
                                continue
                            if fields.get("word_count", 0) >= MIN_WORD_COUNT and fields.get("clean_text"):
                                # Drop raw_bytes before yield: the driver needs only
                                # extracted fields + metadata, not the full HTML.
                                # file_bytes is the absolute wire offset: the stored
                                # checkpoint for byte-range resume.
                                yield {
                                    "url": raw_rec.get("url", ""),
                                    "warc_date": raw_rec.get("warc_date"),
                                    "record_id": raw_rec.get("record_id", ""),
                                    "title": fields.get("title", "N/A"),
                                    "author": fields.get("author", "N/A"),
                                    "published_date": fields.get("published_date", "N/A"),
                                    "clean_text": fields.get("clean_text", ""),
                                    "word_count": fields.get("word_count", 0),
                                    "char_count": fields.get("char_count", 0),
                                    "links_count": fields.get("links_count", 0),
                                    "headings_sample": fields.get("headings_sample", []),
                                    "extraction": fields.get("extraction", "selectolax"),
                                    "file_url": file_url,
                                    "file_bytes": wire_base + counter.bytes_read,
                                }
                                file_count += 1
                                if chunk_cap is not None and file_count >= chunk_cap:
                                    capped = True
                                    break
                        # EOF (complete) only when the stream was consumed to the
                        # known end without a cap break or an error. Unknown size
                        # keeps the legacy silent-end semantics.
                        consumed = wire_base + counter.bytes_read
                        eof = not capped and (
                            total_size is None or consumed >= total_size - 1
                        )
                        yield {
                            "__file_done__": True,
                            "file_url": file_url,
                            "file_bytes": consumed,
                            "eof": eof,
                        }
                        break
                    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
                        if error.code == 416 and not range_failed:
                            # Stored offset past EOF (file shrank / overshoot):
                            # retry once as a full GET.
                            print(f"Range unsatisfiable for {file_url}, falling back to full GET.")
                            range_failed = True
                            try:
                                if response is not None:
                                    response.close()
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                            continue
                        print(f"Download/parse of {file_url} failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {error}")
                        if attempt >= MAX_RETRIES - 1 or not _is_transient_download_error(error):
                            print(f"SKIP {file_url}: {error}")
                            break
                        _sleep_backoff(attempt)
                    except Exception as error:
                        print(f"Download/parse of {file_url} failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{MAX_RETRIES}): {error}")
                        if attempt >= MAX_RETRIES - 1 or not _is_transient_download_error(error):
                            print(f"SKIP {file_url}: {error}")
                            break
                        _sleep_backoff(attempt)
                    finally:
                        try:
                            if response is not None:
                                response.close()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
    
        print(
            f"Starting Streaming Pipeline into PostgreSQL | files={len(warc_urls)} "
            f"chunk_per_round={'unlimited' if chunk_cap is None else chunk_cap} (extracted) "
            f"max_accepted_per_file={'unlimited' if MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE <= 0 else MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE} "
            f"max_accepted={'unlimited' if MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES <= 0 else MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES}"
        )
        start_time = time.perf_counter()
    
        stats = {"extracted": 0, "accepted": 0, "stored": 0, "rejected": 0, "skipped_saved": 0, "dead_letter": 0,
                 "via_trafilatura": 0, "via_fallback": 0}
        file_accepted = {}  # file_url -> accepted articles this run
        resumed_files = set()
        files_done = set()
        seen_ids: set = set()
        db_batch = []
        limit_reached = False
    
        # Progress bar (stderr; auto-disabled when piped). Updated per batch
        # flush, not per record. Tracks accepted articles when capped, extracted
        # articles when uncapped (accepted would sit at 0 with no denominator).
        bar = None
        bar_shown = 0
        bar_key = "accepted" if MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES > 0 else "extracted"
        if USE_PROGRESS_BAR:
            try:
                from tqdm import tqdm
                bar = tqdm(
                    total=MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES if MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES > 0 else None,
                    unit="art",
                    desc=bar_key,
                    dynamic_ncols=True,
                )
            except Exception as error:
                print(f"Progress bar disabled ({error}).")
                bar = None
    
        def _refresh_bar() -> None:
            nonlocal bar_shown
            if bar is None:
                return
            delta = stats[bar_key] - bar_shown
            if delta > 0:
                bar.update(delta)
                bar_shown = stats[bar_key]
            bar.set_postfix(
                {
                    "files": f"{len(files_done)}/{len(warc_urls)}",
                    "extr": stats["extracted"],
                    "rej": stats["rejected"],
                    "skip": stats["skipped_saved"],
                    "saved": stats["stored"],
                },
                refresh=False,
            )
            bar.refresh()
    
        def _consume(record_iter) -> None:
            """Drain one round's iterator: batch, flush, checkpoint, bar."""
            nonlocal db_batch, limit_reached
            try:
                for record in record_iter:
                    if record.get("__file_done__"):
                        # File boundary: flush first, then commit (crash-safe order).
                        furl = record.get("file_url", "")
                        if db_batch:
                            db_batch = _flush_batch(db_batch, seen_ids, stats, file_accepted)
                            _commit_progress()
                            _refresh_bar()
                        if furl:
                            _commit_progress({furl: bool(record.get("eof", False))})
                            _refresh_bar()
                        continue
                    stats["extracted"] += 1
                    clean_text = record.get("clean_text", "")
                    url = record.get("url", "")
                    furl = record.get("file_url", "")
                    if furl:
                        pending_new[furl] = pending_new.get(furl, 0) + 1
                        fbytes = record.get("file_bytes", 0) or 0
                        if fbytes > pending_bytes.get(furl, 0):
                            pending_bytes[furl] = fbytes
                        touched.add(furl)
                    db_batch.append({
                        "record_id": make_record_id(record, url, clean_text),
                        "url": url,
                        "file_url": furl,
                        "title": record.get("title", "N/A"),
                        "author": record.get("author", "N/A"),
                        "published_date": record.get("published_date", "N/A"),
                        "cleaned_text": clean_text,
                        "word_count": record.get("word_count", 0),
                        "char_count": record.get("char_count", 0),
                        "links_count": record.get("links_count", 0),
                        "headings": record.get("headings_sample", []),
                        "arabic_dialect": "MSA",
                        "extraction": record.get("extraction", "selectolax"),
                    })
                    if record.get("extraction") == "trafilatura":
                        stats["via_trafilatura"] += 1
                    else:
                        stats["via_fallback"] += 1
    
                    if MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES > 0 and stats["accepted"] >= MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES:
                        limit_reached = True
                        break
    
                    if len(db_batch) >= DB_BATCH_SIZE:
                        db_batch = _flush_batch(db_batch, seen_ids, stats, file_accepted)
                        _commit_progress()
                        if MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE > 0:
                            for url, n in file_accepted.items():
                                if n >= MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE:
                                    files_done.add(url)
                        _refresh_bar()
            finally:
                if db_batch:
                    _flush_batch(db_batch, seen_ids, stats, file_accepted)
                    _commit_progress()
                    _refresh_bar()
                if limit_reached:
                    print("Accepted-article limit reached, cancelling remaining Spark tasks...")
                    try:
                        sc.cancelAllJobs()
                    except Exception as error:
                        print(f"cancelAllJobs failed (workers wind down on their own): {error}")
    
        # Rounds over the unfinished-file set: each round submits at most
        # MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND files as parallel partitions, each resuming
        # from its byte checkpoint with a bounded transfer chunk
        # (MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND extracted). Files hitting their accepted cap or
        # EOF drop out of the next round's set.
        unfinished = list(warc_urls)
        file_stalls: dict = {}
        file_rounds: dict = {}
        while unfinished and not limit_reached:
            active = []
            for url in unfinished:
                entry = stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))
                if entry[2]:
                    files_done.add(url)
                    continue
                if MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE > 0 and file_accepted.get(url, 0) >= MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE:
                    print(f"Per-file accepted cap reached for {url}, moving on.")
                    files_done.add(url)
                    continue
                active.append(url)
            if not active:
                break
            # One round covers only the first N unfinished files: bounds
            # in-flight partitions and task-result blocks for any manifest size.
            round_files = (
                active[:MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND]
                if MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND > 0 else active
            )
            round_resume = {}
            for url in round_files:
                entry = stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))
                if entry[0] > 0:
                    round_resume[url] = (max(0, entry[0] - overlap), entry[1])
            round_state["resume"] = round_resume
            if round_resume:
                resumed_files.update(round_resume)
            before = {
                url: (stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))[0],
                      stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))[1])
                for url in round_files
            }
            round_rdd = sc.parallelize(round_files, numSlices=len(round_files)).mapPartitions(process_partition)
            try:
                _consume(round_rdd.toLocalIterator())
            except Exception as error:
                # Stage abort (e.g. repeated TaskResultLost): checkpoints
                # committed so far are preserved; stop scheduling new rounds.
                print(f"Round failed, stopping scheduling (progress kept): {type(error).__name__}: {error}")
                limit_reached = True
                break
            if limit_reached:
                break
            # Files outside this round's slice carry over untouched.
            in_round = set(round_files)
            still = [url for url in unfinished if url not in in_round]
            for url in round_files:
                after = stored_progress.get(url, (0, 0, False))
                if after[2]:
                    files_done.add(url)
                    continue
                if MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE > 0 and file_accepted.get(url, 0) >= MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE:
                    files_done.add(url)
                    continue
                if (after[0], after[1]) == before[url]:
                    file_stalls[url] = file_stalls.get(url, 0) + 1
                    if file_stalls[url] >= 3:
                        print(f"No checkpoint progress for {url} after 3 rounds, moving on.")
                        continue
                else:
                    file_stalls[url] = 0
                file_rounds[url] = file_rounds.get(url, 0) + 1
                if file_rounds[url] > 1000:
                    print(f"Round budget exhausted for {url}, moving on.")
                    continue
                still.append(url)
            unfinished = still
    
        if bar is not None:
            bar.close()
    
        if spark is not None:
            spark.stop()
        print(
            f"\nCompleted Streaming Processing! Extracted: {stats['extracted']:,}, "
            f"via trafilatura: {stats['via_trafilatura']:,}, via fallback: {stats['via_fallback']:,}, "
            f"Skipped (already saved): {stats['skipped_saved']:,}, "
            f"Rejected: {stats['rejected']:,}, Accepted: {stats['accepted']:,}, "
            f"Saved: {stats['stored']:,} records, "
            f"dead-letter batches: {stats['dead_letter']}, "
            f"resumed files: {len(resumed_files):,}, checkpoint-skipped: {skipped_complete:,} "
            f"in {time.perf_counter() - start_time:.2f}s"
        )
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        run_streaming_pipeline()

    """Distributed, bounded-memory CC-NEWS ingestion into PostgreSQL.
    
    Workers download, parse, extract, and classify. The driver is the single
    transaction coordinator and counts only rows PostgreSQL actually inserts.
    """
    
    import gzip
    import hashlib
    import json
    import math
    import os
    import socket
    import sys
    import time
    import urllib.error
    import urllib.request
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    
    from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
    
    from db.db_handler import (
        acquire_coordinator_lock,
        commit_chunk,
        create_run,
        finish_run,
        init_db,
        load_run,
        load_run_files,
    )
    from extractors.extractors import detect_languages_batch, extract_html_fields
    from parsers.parsers import parse_warc_records_streaming
    from utils import is_target_language
    
    
    def _env_int(name, default, *, minimum=0):
        raw = os.environ.get(name, str(default)).strip()
        try:
            value = int(raw)
        except ValueError as error:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be an integer, got {raw!r}") from error
        if value < minimum:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must be at least {minimum}, got {value}")
        return value
    
    
    def _parse_num_files(raw):
        text = (raw or "15").strip().lower()
        if text in ("", "all", "none", "0"):
            return None
        try:
            value = int(text)
        except ValueError as error:
            raise ValueError(f"NUM_FILES must be a positive integer or 'all', got {raw!r}") from error
        if value < 1:
            raise ValueError("NUM_FILES must be positive, 0, or 'all'")
        return value
    
    
    def load_config():
        if "MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND" in os.environ:
            raise ValueError(
                "MAX_EXTRACTED_PER_ROUND was removed; use WORKER_MAX_CANDIDATES, "
                "WORKER_MAX_RESULT_BYTES, and WORKER_MAX_INPUT_BYTES"
            )
        year = os.environ.get("YEAR", "2026").strip()
        month = os.environ.get("MONTH", "05").strip().zfill(2)
        if not (year.isdigit() and len(year) == 4 and month.isdigit() and 1 <= int(month) <= 12):
            raise ValueError("YEAR/MONTH must identify a valid YYYY/MM manifest")
        config = {
            "year": year,
            "month": month,
            "manifest_id": f"{year}/{month}",
            "num_files": _parse_num_files(os.environ.get("NUM_FILES")),
            "start_file_offset": _env_int("START_FILE_OFFSET", 0),
            "target_articles": _env_int("MAX_ACCEPTED_ARTICLES", 0),
            "per_file_target": _env_int("MAX_ACCEPTED_PER_FILE", 0),
            "max_files_per_round": _env_int("MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND", 3, minimum=1),
            "worker_max_candidates": _env_int("WORKER_MAX_CANDIDATES", 100, minimum=1),
            "worker_max_result_bytes": _env_int("WORKER_MAX_RESULT_BYTES", 4 * 1024 * 1024, minimum=1024),
            "worker_max_input_bytes": _env_int("WORKER_MAX_INPUT_BYTES", 32 * 1024 * 1024, minimum=65536),
            "worker_max_seconds": _env_int("WORKER_MAX_SECONDS", 120, minimum=1),
            "max_html_bytes": _env_int("MAX_HTML_BYTES", 2 * 1024 * 1024, minimum=1024),
            "max_article_bytes": _env_int("MAX_ARTICLE_BYTES", 512 * 1024, minimum=1024),
            "min_word_count": _env_int("MIN_WORD_COUNT", 80, minimum=1),
            "max_retries": _env_int("MAX_RETRIES", 3, minimum=1),
            "db_batch_size": _env_int("DB_BATCH_SIZE", 25, minimum=1),
            "resume_run_id": os.environ.get("RESUME_RUN_ID", "").strip(),
            "no_progress": os.environ.get("NO_PROGRESS", "0") == "1",
            "dry_run": os.environ.get("DRY_RUN", "0") == "1",
        }
        if config["max_files_per_round"] > 3:
            print("WARNING: MAX_FILES_PER_ROUND exceeds the cluster's three executors; using 3.")
            config["max_files_per_round"] = 3
        return config
    
    
    def get_spark_session():
        builder = (
            SparkSession.builder.appName("CC-NEWS-Bounded-Ingest")
            .config("spark.task.maxDirectResultSize", "16m")
            .config("spark.python.worker.reuse", "true")
        )
        if os.environ.get("SPARK_MASTER_URL"):
            builder = builder.master(os.environ["SPARK_MASTER_URL"])
        return builder.getOrCreate()
    
    
    def _sleep_backoff(attempt):
        time.sleep(min(30, 2 ** (attempt + 1)))
    
    
    def _transient(error):
        if isinstance(error, (socket.timeout, TimeoutError, ConnectionError, urllib.error.URLError)):
            return True
        return isinstance(error, urllib.error.HTTPError) and (error.code == 429 or 500 <= error.code < 600)
    
    
    def fetch_manifest(config):
        url = (
            f"https://data.commoncrawl.org/crawl-data/CC-NEWS/"
            f"{config['year']}/{config['month']}/warc.paths.gz"
        )
        last_error = None
        for attempt in range(config["max_retries"]):
            try:
                request = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "spark-warc/2"})
                with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=60) as response:
                    paths = gzip.decompress(response.read()).decode("utf-8").splitlines()
                window = paths[config["start_file_offset"]:]
                selected = window if config["num_files"] is None else window[:config["num_files"]]
                return [f"https://data.commoncrawl.org/{path}" for path in selected]
            except Exception as error:
                last_error = error
                if attempt + 1 == config["max_retries"] or not _transient(error):
                    raise
                _sleep_backoff(attempt)
        raise last_error
    
    
    def _record_id(record, clean_text):
        if record.get("record_id"):
            return record["record_id"]
        basis = record.get("url", "") + clean_text[:500]
        return "gen-" + hashlib.sha256(basis.encode("utf-8", "ignore")).hexdigest()
    
    
    def _candidate(record, fields, language, file_url):
        text = fields["clean_text"].replace("\x00", "")
        return {
            "record_id": _record_id(record, text),
            "url": record.get("url", "").replace("\x00", ""),
            "file_url": file_url,
            "warc_date": record.get("warc_date"),
            "title": str(fields.get("title") or "N/A").replace("\x00", ""),
            "author": str(fields.get("author") or "N/A").replace("\x00", ""),
            "published_date": str(fields.get("published_date") or "N/A").replace("\x00", ""),
            "cleaned_text": text,
            "word_count": fields.get("word_count", 0),
            "char_count": fields.get("char_count", len(text)),
            "links_count": fields.get("links_count", 0),
            "headings": fields.get("headings_sample", []),
            "language": language,
            "arabic_dialect": None,
            "extraction": fields.get("extraction", "selectolax"),
        }
    
    
    def _content_total(response, offset, ranged):
        if ranged:
            content_range = response.headers.get("Content-Range", "")
            if "/" in content_range:
                total = content_range.rsplit("/", 1)[1]
                if total.isdigit():
                    return int(total)
        length = response.headers.get("Content-Length")
        if length and length.isdigit():
            return offset + int(length) if ranged else int(length)
        return None
    
    
    def _process_file_once(task):
        file_url = task["file_url"]
        start_offset = task["start_offset"]
        headers = {"User-Agent": "spark-warc/2"}
        if start_offset:
            headers["Range"] = f"bytes={start_offset}-"
        request = urllib.request.Request(file_url, headers=headers)
        started = time.monotonic()
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=120) as response:
            status = getattr(response, "status", 200)
            ranged = start_offset > 0 and status == 206
            base_offset = start_offset if ranged else 0
            if ranged:
                content_range = response.headers.get("Content-Range", "")
                if not content_range.startswith(f"bytes {start_offset}-"):
                    raise IOError(f"unexpected Content-Range: {content_range!r}")
            etag = response.headers.get("ETag")
            modified = response.headers.get("Last-Modified")
            if task.get("etag") and etag and task["etag"] != etag:
                raise IOError("source ETag changed since the previous chunk")
            if task.get("last_modified") and modified and task["last_modified"] != modified:
                raise IOError("source Last-Modified changed since the previous chunk")
            total_size = _content_total(response, base_offset, ranged)
            records = iter(parse_warc_records_streaming(
                response,
                max_html_bytes=task["max_html_bytes"],
                base_offset=base_offset,
            ))
            try:
                current = next(records)
            except StopIteration:
                return {
                    "chunk_id": task["chunk_id"], "file_url": file_url,
                    "start_offset": start_offset, "next_offset": total_size or start_offset,
                    "candidates": [], "eof": True, "etag": etag,
                    "last_modified": modified, "stats": {"records": 0}, "error": None,
                }
    
            candidates, result_bytes = [], 0
            stats = {"records": 0, "non_html": 0, "oversized_html": 0,
                     "too_short_or_corrupt": 0, "language_rejected": 0,
                     "oversized_article": 0, "accepted_candidates": 0}
            seen = set()
            while current is not None:
                try:
                    following = next(records)
                    next_offset = following["start_offset"]
                    clean_eof = False
                except StopIteration:
                    following = None
                    next_offset = total_size
                    clean_eof = True
                if current["start_offset"] < start_offset:
                    current = following
                    continue
                stats["records"] += 1
                skip = current.get("skip")
                if skip:
                    stats[skip] = stats.get(skip, 0) + 1
                else:
                    try:
                        fields = extract_html_fields(current, task["min_word_count"])
                    except Exception:
                        fields = {}
                    if not fields.get("clean_text") or fields.get("word_count", 0) < task["min_word_count"]:
                        stats["too_short_or_corrupt"] += 1
                    else:
                        language = detect_languages_batch([fields["clean_text"]])[0]
                        if not is_target_language(language):
                            stats["language_rejected"] += 1
                        else:
                            item = _candidate(current, fields, language, file_url)
                            encoded_size = len(json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False, default=str).encode("utf-8"))
                            if encoded_size > task["max_article_bytes"]:
                                stats["oversized_article"] += 1
                            elif item["record_id"] not in seen and result_bytes + encoded_size <= task["max_result_bytes"]:
                                seen.add(item["record_id"])
                                candidates.append(item)
                                result_bytes += encoded_size
                                stats["accepted_candidates"] += 1
                elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
                consumed = (next_offset if next_offset is not None else current["start_offset"]) - start_offset
                stop = (
                    len(candidates) >= task["candidate_limit"]
                    or result_bytes >= task["max_result_bytes"]
                    or consumed >= task["max_input_bytes"]
                    or elapsed >= task["max_seconds"]
                )
                if stop or clean_eof:
                    if next_offset is None:
                        raise IOError("cannot prove EOF because source size is unknown")
                    stats["result_bytes"] = result_bytes
                    stats["elapsed_seconds"] = round(elapsed, 3)
                    return {
                        "chunk_id": task["chunk_id"], "file_url": file_url,
                        "start_offset": start_offset, "next_offset": next_offset,
                        "candidates": candidates, "eof": clean_eof,
                        "etag": etag, "last_modified": modified,
                        "stats": stats, "error": None,
                    }
                current = following
        raise IOError("stream ended without a checkpoint result")
    
    
    def process_file_chunk(task):
        """Spark worker entry point with bounded retry and no external writes."""
        last_error = None
        for attempt in range(task["max_retries"]):
            try:
                return _process_file_once(task)
            except Exception as error:
                last_error = error
                if attempt + 1 == task["max_retries"] or not _transient(error):
                    break
                _sleep_backoff(attempt)
        return {
            "chunk_id": task["chunk_id"], "file_url": task["file_url"],
            "start_offset": task["start_offset"], "next_offset": task["start_offset"],
            "candidates": [], "eof": False, "etag": task.get("etag"),
            "last_modified": task.get("last_modified"), "stats": {},
            "error": f"{type(last_error).__name__}: {last_error}",
        }
    
    
    def _allocate_tasks(config, run, files, wave):
        active = [f for f in files if f["status"] == "pending" and not f["eof"]]
        active = active[:config["max_files_per_round"]]
        if not active:
            return []
        remaining_total = None if run["target_articles"] == 0 else run["target_articles"] - run["inserted_count"]
        tasks = []
        for index, file_state in enumerate(active):
            slots_left = len(active) - index
            if remaining_total is None:
                allowance = config["worker_max_candidates"]
            else:
                allowance = min(config["worker_max_candidates"], max(1, math.ceil(remaining_total / slots_left)))
            if run["per_file_target"]:
                allowance = min(allowance, run["per_file_target"] - file_state["inserted_count"])
            if allowance <= 0:
                continue
            chunk_key = f"{run['run_id']}\0{file_state['file_url']}\0{file_state['next_offset']}\0{wave}"
            tasks.append({
                "chunk_id": hashlib.sha256(chunk_key.encode()).hexdigest(),
                "file_url": file_state["file_url"], "start_offset": file_state["next_offset"],
                "etag": file_state["etag"], "last_modified": file_state["last_modified"],
                "candidate_limit": allowance,
                "max_result_bytes": config["worker_max_result_bytes"],
                "max_input_bytes": config["worker_max_input_bytes"],
                "max_seconds": config["worker_max_seconds"],
                "max_html_bytes": config["max_html_bytes"],
                "max_article_bytes": config["max_article_bytes"],
                "min_word_count": config["min_word_count"],
                "max_retries": config["max_retries"],
            })
            if remaining_total is not None:
                remaining_total -= allowance
                if remaining_total <= 0:
                    break
        return tasks
    
    
    def _print_config(config):
        printable = {k: v for k, v in config.items() if k not in ("resume_run_id", "no_progress", "dry_run")}
        print(json.dumps(printable, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    
    
    def run_streaming_pipeline():
        config = load_config()
        if config["dry_run"]:
            _print_config(config)
            return 0
    
        init_db()
        lock_conn = acquire_coordinator_lock(config["manifest_id"])
        spark = None
        try:
            if config["resume_run_id"]:
                run = load_run(config["resume_run_id"])
                if run["manifest_id"] != config["manifest_id"]:
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"RESUME_RUN_ID belongs to {run['manifest_id']}; set YEAR/MONTH to match"
                    )
                # Filtering semantics are fixed for a run; resource limits may be tuned on resume.
                stored = run.get("config") or {}
                for key in ("min_word_count",):
                    if key in stored:
                        config[key] = stored[key]
                run_id = run["run_id"]
                print(f"Resuming run {run_id}: {run['inserted_count']}/{run['target_articles'] or 'unlimited'} committed")
            else:
                urls = fetch_manifest(config)
                if not urls:
                    print("No WARC files were selected.")
                    return 2
                persisted = {"min_word_count": config["min_word_count"], "year": config["year"], "month": config["month"]}
                run_id = create_run(config["manifest_id"], urls, config["target_articles"], config["per_file_target"], persisted)
                run = load_run(run_id)
                print(f"Created ingestion run {run_id}")
            print(f"Resume command: RESUME_RUN_ID={run_id} YEAR={config['year']} MONTH={config['month']} ./run_to_db.sh")
    
            spark = get_spark_session()
            sc = spark.sparkContext
            sc.setLogLevel("WARN")
            wave = 0
            totals = {"candidates": 0, "inserted": 0, "failed_files": 0}
            while True:
                run = load_run(run_id)
                files = load_run_files(run_id)
                if run["target_articles"] and run["inserted_count"] >= run["target_articles"]:
                    finish_run(run_id, "complete")
                    print(f"Target reached exactly: {run['inserted_count']:,} new articles committed.")
                    return 0
                tasks = _allocate_tasks(config, run, files, wave)
                if not tasks:
                    status = "complete" if run["target_articles"] == 0 else "shortfall"
                    finish_run(run_id, status)
                    short = max(0, run["target_articles"] - run["inserted_count"])
                    print(f"Sources exhausted: committed {run['inserted_count']:,}; shortfall {short:,}.")
                    return 0 if status == "complete" else 2
                wave += 1
                results = sc.parallelize(tasks, len(tasks)).map(process_file_chunk).collect()
                made_progress = False
                for result in results:
                    if result.get("error"):
                        print(f"FAILED {result['file_url']}: {result['error']}")
                        totals["failed_files"] += 1
                    inserted = None
                    for attempt in range(config["max_retries"]):
                        try:
                            inserted = commit_chunk(run_id, result)
                            break
                        except Exception as error:
                            if attempt + 1 == config["max_retries"]:
                                finish_run(run_id, "failed")
                                raise RuntimeError(f"database commit failed; run {run_id} is resumable") from error
                            _sleep_backoff(attempt)
                    totals["candidates"] += len(result["candidates"])
                    totals["inserted"] += inserted
                    made_progress |= result["next_offset"] > result["start_offset"] or inserted > 0
                run = load_run(run_id)
                print(
                    f"Wave {wave}: committed {run['inserted_count']:,}/"
                    f"{run['target_articles'] or 'unlimited'}; "
                    f"wave candidates={sum(len(r['candidates']) for r in results):,}, "
                    f"new={sum(commit_chunk(run_id, r) for r in results):,}"
                )
                if not made_progress:
                    finish_run(run_id, "failed")
                    raise RuntimeError(f"no cursor or insert progress in wave {wave}; run {run_id} stopped safely")
        finally:
            if spark is not None:
                spark.stop()
            lock_conn.close()
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        try:
            sys.exit(run_streaming_pipeline())
        except Exception as error:
            stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            print(f"[{stamp}] FATAL {type(error).__name__}: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(1)

## Assistant

The new driver loop now sends bounded work to all three executors concurrently and commits each result as one database transaction. Global and per-file quotas are based on newly inserted rows, so duplicates and language rejects no longer spend the requested count. Resume state is tied to a printed run ID and uses the same environment-variable style as the existing project.
